# MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES requirements

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R001` | **Cross-host BacktestRun digest and Pine parity for finance-core.** The BacktestRun content digest is canonical across supported host architectures. Serialization, floating-point and timestamp inputs are normalized before hashing. A Pine companion script covers the SuperTrend and ATR kernels. It produces the same signal transitions as the Rust implementation. Both read a shared committed bar fixture and parameter version. Finance-specific classes fold into one finance-v1 ontology. Duplicate class definitions are deleted. Production consumer: arbora `market.backtest_run` (ARBORA-DATA-MARKET-R003). Caller chain: the arbora operation `market.backtest_run` (ARBORA-DATA-MARKET-R003) → served `Method::FinanceMarket { op: BacktestRun }` → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs::route_gateway_and_stateless_domains` (:40) (finance arm :98) → `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/finance.rs::try_handle` (:22) → `finance/routes.rs::try_handle` (:137) → `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/finance/market.rs::handle_market` (:92) → `handle` (:75, `FinanceMarketOp::BacktestRun`) → `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/backtest_run.rs::seal` (:186) → `digest::of_json` (`crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/digest.rs`). The Pine companion is not served; it is replayed against the same fixture by `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/pine_parity_tests.rs` and `tests/test_finance_pine_companion.py`. Shape: the digest is `digest::of_json(RUN_DOMAIN, &(draft, &validation))` in `backtest_run.rs::seal`; the sealed `BacktestRun` runs on the pinned soft-float kernel in `crates/medulla-numeric` so no host float behaviour reaches the hash. The pinned digest constant `PINNED_RUN_DIGEST` and the cross-host test live in `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/record_tests.rs` (:407). The Pine companion is `crates/medulla-finance-market/tests/fixtures/finance_market/supertrend_companion.pine`; the shared bar fixture and parameter version are `golden.json` and `pine_parity.json` in the same directory. Finance classes live once in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.ttl`; reuse `digest::of_json`, add no second hasher. | Test: `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/record_tests.rs::a_sealed_backtest_run_digest_is_pinned_across_hosts` (L1) asserts the sealed digest equals the pinned constant; the lane runs it unchanged on two architectures and the digests are compared. `tests/test_finance_pine_companion.py::test_the_companion_states_each_reference_rule` (L1) pins the Pine recurrence rules to the reference, and `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/golden_tests.rs` replays the shared fixture for the Rust side. The class census of one canonical IRI per finance concept is `crates/medulla-h-admin/src/graph_schema/finance_tests.rs::every_finance_class_is_mapped_and_nothing_external_is_imported`. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002` | **Rehome generic finance evaluation kernels into medulla-numeric.** Stage: numeric kernels. Entry points: `NumericEvaluate`, the UQL `DERIVE`, `SKILL` and `EVALUATE` stages, and the `Decide` and `FinanceMarket` handlers. Caller chain: `Method::NumericEvaluate` → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs::route_gateway_and_stateless_domains` (:40) → a new `numeric::try_handle` arm beside the finance arm (:98) → `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/numeric.rs::handle_numeric_evaluate` (new) → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/registry.rs::evaluate` (new); the UQL `DERIVE`, `SKILL` and `EVALUATE` stages reach the same registry through `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/dispatch.rs::apply_timeseries` (:469); `Decide` and `FinanceMarket` reach the kernels through `crates/medulla-h-decide/src/decide/stat_walk_forward.rs::validation` (:171) and `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/backtest_run.rs::validation_outputs` (:153). Rule: medulla-numeric owns 14 kernels. They are rolling z-score, EWMA, information coefficient, information ratio and effective sample size. They also include Diebold-Mariano, deflated Sharpe, purged CV, PBO, Brier, Kalman, OU, ADF and regime HMM. The `Finance<Kernel>` wire methods and the wrappers in `crates/medulla-finance-market` and `crates/medulla-finance-quant` are deleted in the same release. No alias remains. Refusal: bad inputs return `INVALID_ARGUMENT` with a stable `KERNEL_*` code first. Depends: R002.1 (kernels), R002.2 (routes), R002.3 (clients), R002.4 to R002.11 (filters and forecasts), `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R179` (knobs). | Rollup. Closes when R002.1 to R002.11 verify. Proof level L3 in `tests/finance_numeric_evaluate_served.rs::all_kernels_served_and_no_legacy_method` (planned). It drives each kernel through signed dispatch and asserts no `Finance<Kernel>` method decodes. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.1` | **Complete the original shared-kernel inventory.** Rollup of R002.1.1 to R002.1.14. Stage: numeric kernels. Caller chain: `NumericEvaluate` → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/registry.rs::evaluate` (new, R002.2.5) → the kernel; census guard `crates/medulla-numeric/tests/kernel_inventory.rs` (new). Rule: each kernel has one implementation under `crates/medulla-numeric/src/`. R002.1.1 to R002.1.10 are in place. R002.1.11 to R002.1.14 move Kalman, OU, ADF and the regime HMM out of `crates/medulla-finance-quant`. Refusal: duplicate implementations fail the census in R002.2.4. Depends: R002.2.5 (registry). | Rollup. Closes when R002.1.1 to R002.1.14 verify. Proof level L1 in `crates/medulla-numeric/tests/kernel_inventory.rs::one_implementation_per_kernel` (planned), which greps `medulla-compute` for the moved function names. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.1.1` | **Rolling z-score shared kernel.** Caller chain: `NumericEvaluate` op `rolling_z_score` (R002.2.5) → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/registry.rs::evaluate` (new, R002.2.5) → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kernel.rs` (`zscore` at :189, selected by `Spec::Rolling(Rolling::Zscore, window)` in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/mod.rs`, run through `apply_nan` at :233). Today the same kernel is reached through UQL `DERIVE zscore(v0, w)` → `crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/mod.rs` (`BUILDS`, `F::Zscore`) and `crates/medulla-tsdb/src/query.rs::series_rolling_zscore` (:294) → `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/signals.rs::rolling_zscore` (:18). Model: The Rolling z-score inputs and numeric policy retain their original meaning. Behavior: The shared authority computes only this kernel. Reuse an existing implementation when present. Shape: no new kernel code: the kernel above is the one implementation and keeps its typed-error form; the registry arm only maps the wire fields of the op to its arguments and the typed `Error` to a `KERNEL_*` code. Reuse it, add no copy. Its test `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kernel.rs::tests::rolling_z_score_independent_vectors` (:282) already exists. Refusal: Malformed, nonfinite and insufficient inputs produce typed outcomes. Evidence: Independent fixed vectors match this kernel and its declared precision. | Test: `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kernel.rs::tests::rolling_z_score_independent_vectors`. Independent fixed vectors match this kernel and its declared precision. Malformed and short vectors refuse; a duplicate authority fails qualification. |

Today the served path is `Method::FinanceKalmanFilter1d`, `FinanceKalmanBeta`, `FinanceKalmanVolatility` → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs::route_gateway_and_stateless_domains` (:40) (finance arm :98) → `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/finance.rs::try_handle` (:22) → `finance/routes.rs::try_handle` (:137) → `finance/route_families.rs::handle_state_space` (:639, arms :642, :665, :683) → `crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/statespace.rs::kalman_filter_1d` (:56), `kalman_beta` (:73), `kalman_volatility` (:94). Shape: `Op::Evaluate { spec: EvaluateOp }` in `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_query_core.rs` beside `Op::Skill` (:733), with `EvaluateOp` in `crates/medulla-types/src/series_expr.rs` beside `SkillOp` (:243); grammar in `crates/medulla-uql/src/parser/series.rs` beside `skill` (:28), owned by `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R726`; executor `crates/medulla-exec/src/exec/evaluate.rs` modelled on `skill.rs::skill_op` (:19), reusing `derive.rs::named_series_groups` and `rowset.rs::series_row_id` and the series work budget `ctx.budget.max_series_work` that `motif_op` already receives at `dispatch.rs:apply_timeseries`. Caller chain: `TsAppend` → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/dispatch_helpers.rs` (:143, `medulla_h_txn::timeseries::try_handle_with_nonce`) → `crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries.rs::try_handle_with_nonce` (:665, `Method::TsAppend` arm :690) → `handle_append` (:371) → `crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries/derived.rs::maintain_after_append` (:74) → `maintain_one` (:224) → `crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/maintain.rs::DerivedState::advance` (:108) → `Program::step` (`crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/mod.rs:167`); and `NumericEvaluate` → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/registry.rs::evaluate` (new, R002.2.5). Shape: `StateSpaceCeilings` consts in the new `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/limits.rs`; the two ceilings that already exist stay the single definitions and are re-exported, not copied: `MAX_STATE_BYTES` (`crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/maintain.rs:33`, 1 MiB) and `WORK_BUDGET` (`crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries/derived.rs:30`, 100_000 source points, passed to `DerivedState::advance` and `replay`); `CHECKPOINT_EVERY` (`maintain.rs:27`) stays 256. Every limit is checked before allocation, and the refusal path returns before `derived.rs::commit` (:252) so the prior checkpoint stays. Rule: the fixed ceilings are state dimension 16, observation dimension 8 and retained observations 4096. Checkpoint bytes are 1_048_576 (`MAX_STATE_BYTES`). Update work is 1_000_000 multiply-adds. Replay points and HMM refit observations are 100_000 each. A model revision may lower a limit and never raise it. Verify every limit before allocation and report consumed amounts. Knobs: none; ceilings are `Fixed` safety limits. Refusal: `KERNEL_STATE_BUDGET` for state and refit overflow, `KERNEL_WORK_BUDGET` for work and replay overflow. A rejected update keeps the prior checkpoint. Write path: the checkpoint commit in `derived.rs::commit` (:252) is skipped on refusal. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.6.1` | **Shared causal linear recurrence.** Stage: kernel. Caller chain: `derived::maintain_one` (`crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries/derived.rs:224`) → `crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/maintain.rs::DerivedState::advance` (:108) → `Program::step` (`crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/mod.rs:167`) → `StateSpaceFilter::update` (new) in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs`; and `NumericEvaluate` op `kalman_level` → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/registry.rs::evaluate` (new, R002.2.5). Shape: `StateSpaceFilter::update` (new) generalises the scalar `observe` (:53) to the matrix recurrence of plan.md; keep `observe` as the scalar fast path so existing `Spec::KalmanLevel` and `Spec::KalmanBeta` stay bit-identical; the test module is new in the same file. Rule: x_minus = F x and P_minus = F P Fᵀ + Q. Then v = z - H x_minus and S = H P_minus Hᵀ + R. Solve S for K by Cholesky and never invert. Update x = x_minus + K v. Use the Joseph form for P. Pin symmetry tolerance 1e-9 and decomposition tolerance 1e-12 in the model revision. Refusal: singular S returns `KERNEL_SINGULAR`. A nonzero process and measurement cross-covariance returns `STATE_NOISE_CORRELATED` before any update. State is unchanged. Depends: R002.5. | `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs::tests::causal_linear_recurrence_and_noise_refusal` (planned) (L1) matches the scalar rational vectors at every step and checkpoint. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.7` | **Detect innovation shifts and retune within fixed bounds.** Stage: kernel adaptation. Caller chain: `TsAppend` → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/dispatch_helpers.rs` (:143, `medulla_h_txn::timeseries::try_handle_with_nonce`) → `crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries.rs::try_handle_with_nonce` (:665, `Method::TsAppend` arm :690) → `handle_append` (:371) → `crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries/derived.rs::maintain_after_append` (:74) → `maintain_one` (:224) → `crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/maintain.rs::DerivedState::advance` (:108) → `Program::step` (`crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/mod.rs:167`) → `StateSpaceFilter::update` (new) → `StateSpaceFilter::detect_shift` (new) in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs`. Shape: `detect_shift` and the retune live in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs` next to `StateSpaceFilter::update`; the baseline and recent windows are fixed-size ring buffers held in the `Kalman` serde state so they ride in the derived checkpoint (`DerivedState.checkpoints`, `maintain.rs:58`); W, ratio band, confirmation count and cooldown are fields of the model revision (`StateSpaceModel`, R002.5). Rule: whiten each innovation by its own S with a lower-triangular Cholesky solve. Keep a fixed baseline window and a recent window, each W=32. From 64 observations compare recent variance with max(baseline, 1e-12). A ratio outside [0.5, 2.0] for 3 consecutive windows is a shift. Compare every 32 observations. After a shift wait 64 observations, then use the last 32 as the new baseline. Scale R by clamp(ratio, 0.5, 2.0) within the revision's eigenvalue bounds. Keep Q unchanged. Publish old and new parameter digests and the trigger cursor. The new revision applies from the next observation. Knobs: W, ratio band, confirmation count and cooldown are model-revision fields with these defaults and are `Fixed` in this release. Refusal: `STATE_WARMUP`, `STATE_RETUNE_BUDGET` (3 retunes per 1024 observations) and `STATE_WINDOW_INVALID`. Depends: R002.5, R002.6.1. | `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs::tests::bounded_innovation_retuning` (planned) (L1) uses a stable, a shifted and an isolated-outlier sequence. It verifies exact trigger indices, clamps, cooldown and revision timing against an independent seeded oracle. |
Depends: MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.2.5, MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.5
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.8` | **Bound nonlinear approximation and robust tail influence.** Stage: kernel. Caller chain: `TsAppend` → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline/dispatch_helpers.rs` (:143, `medulla_h_txn::timeseries::try_handle_with_nonce`) → `crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries.rs::try_handle_with_nonce` (:665, `Method::TsAppend` arm :690) → `handle_append` (:371) → `crates/medulla-h-txn/src/timeseries/derived.rs::maintain_after_append` (:74) → `maintain_one` (:224) → `crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/maintain.rs::DerivedState::advance` (:108) → `Program::step` (`crates/medulla-tsdb/src/derive/mod.rs:167`) → `StateSpaceFilter::update_robust` (new) in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs`. Shape: `StateSpaceFilter::update_robust` (new) reuses the Joseph-form covariance update of `StateSpaceFilter::update` and computes `w = min(1, c / max(d, 1e-12))` with `c = 2.5`; registered profiles supply `f`, `h` and their Jacobians through a closed enum in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs`, never caller code. Rule: registered profiles supply f, h, finite Jacobians and valid domains; no caller code runs. Linearize once at the prior state. With d = sqrt(vᵀ S⁻¹ v), set w = min(1, c / max(d, 1e-12)) with c = 2.5. Use K_eff = w K in the state update and the Joseph form. Report w, d and the approximation flag. Shift detection uses pre-downweight innovations with each whitened component clipped to [-8, 8]. Refusal: `STATE_MODEL_UNREGISTERED`, `STATE_JACOBIAN_INVALID`, `STATE_DOMAIN_VIOLATION`, `KERNEL_INPUT_NONFINITE`. No optimality claim is made. Depends: R002.6.1, R002.7. | `crates/medulla-numeric/src/series/kalman.rs::tests::nonlinear_robust_gain` (planned) (L1) uses f(x)=x and h(x)=x² on x>0 with derivative 2x, both residual signs and seeded heavy tails. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R003` | **Compute PBO from a BacktestRun's own CSCV splits.** Probability of backtest overfitting comes from a BacktestRun's own combinatorial symmetric cross-validation splits. It never uses a separate caller-supplied summary of performance rows. For each split the implementation selects the top in-sample candidate by a deterministic tie rule. It ranks that candidate's out-of-sample performance against all candidates. It counts the split as overfit only when the rank is strictly below the exact median. Equality to the median is not overfit. Caller chain: signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs::route_gateway_and_stateless_domains` (:40) (finance arm :98) → `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/finance.rs::try_handle` (:22) → `finance/routes.rs::try_handle` (:137) → `finance/market.rs::handle_market` (:92) → `FinanceMarketOp::BacktestRun` → `handle` (:75) → `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/backtest_run.rs::seal` (:186) → `validation_outputs` (:153), which calls `purged_cpcv_splits` (:155) and `probability_of_backtest_overfit` (:181). Shape: `validation_outputs` builds the in-sample and out-of-sample matrices from the record's own `inputs.performance` through `split_performance` over each split's `train` and `test` indices (`backtest_run.rs:153-:183`) and passes them to the one kernel `probability_of_backtest_overfit` (`crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/backtest.rs:113`); the kernel picks the in-sample best with the first-on-ties rule (`oos_rank_of_is_best`, :132) and counts a split only when its out-of-sample rank is strictly below `(columns - 1) / 2`; no caller-supplied summary rows enter. Reuse both, add no second PBO path. | Test: `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/record_tests.rs::pbo_uses_all_cpcv_splits_from_the_records_performance` (L1) asserts the skilled-strategy fixture gives 0 and the overfit fixture gives 1 across all 15 splits, computed from the record's own split matrix, and that the sealed digests differ. A skilled-strategy fixture and an overfit-strategy fixture each assert the expected PBO value computed directly from the record's own split matrix. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R004.2.1` | **Account and Position OWL classes and SHACL shapes.** finance-v1 defines `:Account`, a portfolio owner's brokerage or custodial account. Its fields are `accountId`, `baseCurrency` and `accountKind`. `accountKind` takes `taxable`, `ira`, `roth_ira`, `401k`, `hsa` or `custodial`. finance-v1 also defines `:Position`, an account's signed holding of one instrument under one cost-basis method. Its fields are `positionAccount`, `positionInstrument`, `positionQuantity` and `costBasisMethod`. `costBasisMethod` takes `fifo`, `lifo`, `specific_lot` or `average_cost`. `:Position` differs from the exchange-execution `:PortfolioPosition`. Caller chain: the schema chain of R004.1 → `AccountShape`/`PositionShape` or `ActivityShape`/`LotShape` in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.shapes.ttl` (lines :185 and :204). Code root: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.ttl` and `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. The vocabularies match `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. | SHACL validation tests assert required fields/links are enforced and each closed vocabulary (`accountKind`, `costBasisMethod`) refuses an unlisted value. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R004.2.2` | **Activity and Lot OWL classes and SHACL shapes.** finance-v1 defines `:Activity`, a point-in-time ledger event against a position. `activityKind` takes `buy`, `sell`, `dividend`, `transfer_in`, `transfer_out`, `split` or `fee`. finance-v1 also defines `:Lot`, one cost-basis parcel opened by an acquiring activity. A lot follows the FIFO, LIFO, specific-lot or average-cost method. Caller chain: the schema chain of R004.1 → `ActivityShape` and `LotShape` in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.shapes.ttl` (:228 and :253). Code root: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.ttl` and `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. The vocabularies match `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. | SHACL validation tests assert required fields/links are enforced and each closed vocabulary value is verified. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R004.3.1` | **The wire boundary refuses an unknown finance-v1 schema version.** `admit_finance_record_version` accepts only the current `finance-v1` declared schema version at the wire boundary, refusing an unknown or empty one rather than silently accepting it or guessing a migration. Caller chain: `FinanceRecordPut` and `FinanceRecordGet` → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_records.rs::handle_record` (new) → `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs::admit_finance_record_version` (:62), called before any other record work. Shape: the gate is `admit_finance_record_version` and its constant `FINANCE_V1_SCHEMA_VERSION` (`crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs:46`) returning `FinanceRecordWireRefusal`; the handler maps the refusal to `FINANCE_RECORD_VERSION_UNKNOWN`; reuse the gate, add no second version check. | Test: `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs::finance_record_version_tests::the_current_schema_version_is_admitted` and `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs::finance_record_version_tests::an_unknown_schema_version_is_refused` (L1) assert the current schema version is admitted and an unknown or empty declared version is refused (`an_empty_schema_version_is_refused`). |

Shape: `Method::FinanceRecordPut { kind: FinanceRecordKind, schema_version, record, expected_head }` and `Method::FinanceRecordGet { kind, record_id }` in `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_04.rs`; `FinanceRecordKind` and `FINANCE_RECORD_POLICY` in `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs` beside `admit_finance_record_version`; handler `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_records.rs` (new, gated by the `finance` and `shacl` features of `medulla-h-graph`), not `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/finance/routes.rs`, because that stage is stateless and has no graph core; capability lines in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/domains/` (new `finance.rs`, scope `finance:records`, registered in `domains/mod.rs`); read classification in `crates/medulla-server-auth/src/access/read_classification.rs`; the shape check reuses `medulla_shacl::validate_turtle` against `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. Write path: the handler builds one `MutationBatch` upsert through the mutation gateway used by `AddNode`, after SHACL validation against `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. Refusal: `FINANCE_RECORD_VERSION_UNKNOWN` (from the gate), `RECORD_DERIVED_READONLY`, `ACCESS_DENIED` for a tenant other than the verified one, `RECORD_HEAD_CONFLICT`, `RECORD_SHAPE_INVALID`. The research record entries of R751.754 are further entries of this record module. Depends: R004.3.1, R004.2. | Rollup. Each record child passes `tests/finance_records_served.rs` through signed dispatch. A schema-version unit test alone does not satisfy the rollup. |

Caller chain: signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/plan.rs::is_gateway_routed` (:564; `FinanceRecordPut` is added to `GATEWAY_ROUTED`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway.rs::try_handle_gateway` (:57) → `gateway_graph_routes.rs` (`ROUTE_MATCHERS` :131, new `Route::FinanceRecords`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_records.rs::handle_record` (new) → `FinanceRecordKind::Account` arm → one `MutationBatch` upsert through the gateway, or the graph read of `terminal.rs:20`. Shape: the `Account` arm of `handle_record` consults `FINANCE_RECORD_POLICY` (`crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs`), validates the record against `AccountShape` in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.shapes.ttl` with `medulla_shacl::validate_turtle` and, for a client-writable kind, builds the same `MutationBatch` upsert that `Method::AddNode` uses (`crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway_graph_routes.rs:226`). Rule: `FinanceRecordKind::Account` is client-writable. A valid `Account` put validates the shape, then upserts under the verified tenant with the expected head. A get returns the same fields. Write path: one `MutationBatch` upsert with SHACL validation. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R004.3.2.9` | **Replicated `FinanceRecordPut` command.** Stage: served finance records in cluster mode. Caller chain: signed dispatch → `dispatch_request_method_inner` (`crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs:208`) → `dispatch_graph_op` (`crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs:68`) → `try_handle_gateway` (`crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway.rs:57`) → `finance_records::handle_record` (R004.3.2) → on the leader, the checks, then the proposal of `NativeMutationCommand::FinanceRecordPut` through `crates/medulla-raft/src/raft/command/native/mod.rs`. Rule: in cluster mode a put is no longer refused. The leader runs every check of R004.3.2.1 to R004.3.2.8 before it proposes: the schema version gate, the kind policy (a derived kind refuses `RECORD_DERIVED_READONLY` first), the shape check, the tenant check and the expected-head check. The proposal carries the validated record and `expected_head`; applying it re-reads the stored record head and writes one `MutationBatch` upsert only when it equals `expected_head`, else it answers `RECORD_HEAD_CONFLICT` and writes nothing. `FinanceRecordPut` is a replicated method: it moves from the local-only set to the sealed native consensus catalog (`NATIVE_CONSENSUS_METHODS`), so a clustered dispatch acknowledges it only after replica apply. A follower that receives the put forwards it to the leader. A put that fails a check on the leader proposes nothing and writes nothing. Writes: the finance record node through the one replicated apply path. Depends: R004.3.2. Shape: add a `FinanceRecordPut { sealed_put: SealedNativeMethod }` variant to `NativeMutationCommand` in `crates/medulla-raft/src/raft/command/native/mod.rs` with a new sealed method entry in the native catalog and its deterministic replica apply arm; the sealed body carries `tenant`, `kind: FinanceRecordKind`, `schema_version: u32`, the validated canonical `record` bytes and `expected_head: Option<..>` as in `Method::FinanceRecordPut`; `record` is the validated canonical bytes and is bounded by the existing mutation envelope limit; the exhaustive match of `crates/medulla-server-api/src/batch_compile/canonical.rs` gains the arm; the single-node path applies the same command locally, so there is one apply path. | Test: `tests/finance_records_cluster.rs::replicated_put_checks_on_leader_and_applies_once` (planned) (L3) runs a three-node cluster, asserts a valid put applies on every node, a derived-kind put refuses `RECORD_DERIVED_READONLY` with nothing proposed, and a stale `expected_head` answers `RECORD_HEAD_CONFLICT`. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R005` | **Deterministic fixed-point portfolio accounting.** Portfolio accounting uses fixed-point arithmetic for lot and cost-basis tracking under FIFO, LIFO, specific-lot, and average-cost elections. It derives realized and unrealized profit and loss, time-weighted return, money-weighted return and XIRR. It also derives dividend handling, multi-currency conversion at trade and valuation dates, and benchmark comparison. Delivery is by child requirement, producer first. `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R005.1` is the FIFO/LIFO fixed-point lot-matching engine. `.2` covers specific-lot/average-cost elections and transfer/split invariants. Further children cover P&L/TWR/XIRR/FX/benchmark. Every child must satisfy its acceptance before this parent is complete. | Deterministic unit and golden fixture tests reproduce byte-identical results across replays of the same ordered inputs. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R005.1` | **FIFO/LIFO fixed-point lot-matching engine.** A pure function over an ordered slice of signed fixed-point buy/sell activities matches sells to open lots under FIFO or LIFO, refusing non-positive quantity/price, out-of-order sequence, and oversold quantity. No production caller reaches the lot engine today; its tests are its only callers. Rule: build one `MutationOutboxIntent`. Set `topic` to `finance.events.v1` and `key` to `event_id`. The payload is the canonical msgpack of the envelope. Header `schema` holds the schema name. A flip keeps its existing `finance.flip` event id. The key is the idempotency identity. Refusal: an invalid envelope returns `ALERT_ENVELOPE_INVALID` and compiles nothing. Depends: R011.2.3. Shape: `finance_alert_intent` is a new `pub(super)` fn in `finish_helpers.rs` that returns one `MutationOutboxIntent` (`crates/medulla-mutation-types/src/mutation_scope/outbox.rs:11`: `topic`, `key`, `payload`, `headers`) into `CompiledOutbox.extra`; it reuses `FinanceAlertKind::outbox_event_schema` (`crates/medulla-types/src/finance_event_envelope.rs:32`) and `compiled_outbox` adds the scope and actor headers itself, so the intent carries only `schema`. | `crates/medulla-server-api/src/batch_compile/compile.rs::tests::finance_alert_intents_preserve_schema_and_identity` (planned) (L2) covers the four kinds. `tests/finance_alert_outbox_served.rs::intent_reaches_outbox_with_flip_identity` (planned) (L3) raises through dispatch and reads the outbox fact. |

Caller chain: `FinanceAlertRaise` → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/plan.rs::is_gateway_routed` (:564; the method name is added to `GATEWAY_ROUTED`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway.rs::try_handle_gateway` (:57) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway_graph_routes.rs` (`ROUTE_MATCHERS` :131, new `Route::FinanceRecords`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_alerts.rs::raise` (new) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/commit_entry.rs::commit_mutation` (:264) → one owner transaction. Rule: the event node and the outbox fact commit together or not at all. A conflicting source revision for the same `event_id` refuses. Each committed outbox fact is claimable by queue `finance.alert.<kind>` through `TaskQueueClaim`. Refusal: `ACCESS_DENIED`, `ALERT_REVISION_CONFLICT`. A failed transaction publishes neither record. Write path: owner transaction of `MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R002`. Depends: R011.2.1. Shape: the event node and its outbox intent are one `MutationBatch` (the gateway commit already writes node and outbox rows atomically), and a conflicting source revision is a compare-and-set failure mapped to `ALERT_REVISION_CONFLICT`. `TaskQueueClaim` is not a method in the tree: the claim is `crates/medulla-transaction/src/outbox/claim.rs::claim` (:57) over topic `finance.events.v1`, and the queue name `finance.alert.<kind>` is the consumer name bound to the `schema` header, so no new claim method is added. | `tests/finance_alert_outbox_served.rs::alert_and_outbox_commit_atomically` (planned) (L3) injects a commit failure and asserts neither record exists. Unauthorized tenants and conflicting revisions are refused. A claim by queue `finance.alert.<kind>` returns the committed fact. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R011.3` | **Finance delivery replay.** Stage: outbox replay. Caller chain: replay of a committed `FinanceAlertRaise` and restart recovery → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/plan.rs::is_gateway_routed` (:564; the method name is added to `GATEWAY_ROUTED`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway.rs::try_handle_gateway` (:57) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway_graph_routes.rs` (`ROUTE_MATCHERS` :131, new `Route::FinanceRecords`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_alerts.rs::raise` (new) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/commit_entry.rs::commit_mutation` (:264) replay identity → `crates/medulla-transaction/src/outbox/claim.rs::claim` (:57). Rule: one event id yields one event node and one outbox fact. A second raise with equal bytes returns the first result. All four kinds keep identity across restart. Existing `finance.flip` delivery stays compatible. Refusal: different bytes under the same event id return `ALERT_REVISION_CONFLICT`. An alert never authorises an order. Depends: R011.2.2. Shape: the node id is the envelope `event_id`; an equal-bytes second raise is the gateway's idempotent replay of the first result, and different bytes under the same id are refused `ALERT_REVISION_CONFLICT` before the outbox is touched; the `finance.flip` id keeps its existing form (`finance_alert_intent`, R011.2.1). | `tests/finance_alert_outbox_served.rs::replay_preserves_one_outbox_fact` (planned) (L3) raises each kind twice, restarts the server and counts events and facts. Counts stay one. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.0` | **Pinned regime model record.** Stage: regime forecast. Caller chain: arbora `market.size` model registration → signed dispatch `Method::FinanceRecordPut { kind: RegimeModel }` → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → gateway route `Route::FinanceRecords` → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_records.rs` (`check_record_shape`) → node `finance:RegimeModel:<model_id>`; read back by `Method::FinanceRecordGet` and by `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_regime.rs::read_model` (new). Code: `FinanceRecordKind::RegimeModel` (client-writable) in `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_finance.rs`; class `:RegimeModel` (a BFO generically dependent continuant, rolling up to the core record class) with properties `regimeModelId`, `regimeModelRevision`, `regimeSourceSeriesId`, `regimeSourceRevision`, `regimeAsOf`, `regimeStateIds`, `regimePosterior` and `regimeTransitionMatrix` in `crates/medulla-core/ontology/finance-v1.ttl`, and `:RegimeModelShape` in `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. Rule: the node pins one fitted model at one model revision and one source revision; the state ids, posterior and matrix are JSON text in state order. A put replaces the node under the record head rule of R004.3.2, so an older pinned revision becomes stale. The shape requires all eight properties exactly once; the matrix content is validated at forecast time by R002.11.1. Refusal: a record missing a property is `RECORD_SHAPE_INVALID`; a forecast on an absent node or another tenant's node is `REGIME_MODEL_NOT_FOUND`. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.1` | **Regime forecast p0 times T to the h.** Stage: regime forecast. Caller chain: arbora `market.size` → signed dispatch `Method::RegimeForecast { request }` → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/terminal.rs` (:20, read chain; new `try_handle_finance` arm) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_regime.rs::handle_regime_forecast` (new) → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/evaluation/regime_forecast.rs::forecast` (new, beside the HMM fit of R002.1.14 in `evaluation/regime_hmm.rs`). Code: `RegimeForecastRequest` `{model_id, model_revision, source_series_id, source_revision, as_of, horizons (increasing positive), horizon_unit, calibration (optional, R002.11.2), supplied_p0 and supplied_transition_matrix (must be absent)}` in `crates/medulla-types/src/regime_forecast.rs`. Rule: read ordered state ids, posterior p0 and matrix T only from the pinned HMM model and source revision. Refuse caller-supplied p0 or T. For each h compute p_h = p0 Tʰ and keep state order. Result: `{forecast_digest, revisions, as_of, horizon_unit, horizons, ordered_state_ids, posterior_p0, transition_matrix_digest, per_horizon_probability_vectors, per_horizon_top_state_probability, calibration}`. Knobs: `knob finance.regime_forecast.sum_tolerance` (default 1e-9, probability sums), `knob finance.regime_forecast.max_horizon` (default 10000 steps). The model revision is a field of the pinned node (R002.11.0). A pin is stale when the stored model revision, source series, source revision or as-of differs from the request. Refusal: `FORECAST_MODEL_STALE`, `FORECAST_HORIZON_INVALID` (empty or unsorted), `FORECAST_MATRIX_INVALID` (negative, nonfinite, non-stochastic row, duplicate state ids), `FORECAST_INPUT_SUPPLIED`. Depends: R002.1.14, R002.11.0. This row replaces `MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R092.2`. Shape: the handler reads the pinned HMM model node and the source revision through the graph read path (it needs the graph core, so it lives in `medulla-h-graph` and not in the stateless `medulla-h-compute/src/numeric.rs` of R002.2.5), then calls `forecast`; `RegimeForecastRequest` and the result type sit in `crates/medulla-types/src/regime_forecast.rs` (new in R002.2.5), and `Method::RegimeForecast` sits beside `Method::FinanceFactorRank` in `method_04.rs` (:288); the existing `RegimeForecast` struct of `crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/execution.rs:144` is the unrelated chain forecast of R613 and is not reused. | `tests/finance_regime_forecast_served.rs::forecast_matches_independent_powers` (L3) seeds a three-state matrix and compares independent p0×Tʰ vectors at every horizon, with sums equal to 1. Each refusal code has a test. |
Depends: MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.2.5
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.2` | **Per-horizon categorical Brier calibration gate.** Stage: calibration gate. Caller chain: signed dispatch `Method::RegimeForecast` → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/terminal.rs` (:20, read chain; new `try_handle_finance` arm) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_regime.rs::handle_regime_forecast` (new) → `crates/medulla-numeric/src/calibration/metrics.rs::brier_score` (:159) per horizon. Code: held-out sample `{sample_id, model_revision, source_series_id, origin_as_of, horizon, ordered_state_ids, predicted_probability_vector, realized_state_id, target_observation_at, scoring_revision}`. Rule: Brier_h = (1/N_h) Σ_n Σ_s (p_nhs − 1[y_n=s])². Require origin before target observation, unique sample ids, one row per source, origin and horizon, and matching state order. Policy `RegimeCalibrationPolicy` supplies three values per horizon. They are `max_brier` in [0, 2], `min_top_state_probability` in [0, 1] and a positive integer `minimum_heldout_examples`. Every requested horizon appears once. Result per horizon: `{horizon, dataset_digest, model_revision, state_ids_digest, scoring_revision, calibration_policy_digest, sample_count, brier, applied_thresholds, passed}`. Knobs: `knob finance.regime_forecast.max_brier` is the ceiling of a policy `max_brier` (default and range maximum 2, the largest possible Brier score; unit squared probability); the threshold itself is always supplied by the policy and is never defaulted from the knob. Refusal: `CALIBRATION_ROW_DUPLICATE`, `CALIBRATION_ROW_MISSING`, `CALIBRATION_FUTURE_LEAK`, `CALIBRATION_SAMPLES_LOW`, `CALIBRATION_ROW_MISMATCH` (a row of another model revision, source, state order, horizon or an unknown realized state, or one horizon mixing scoring revisions), `CALIBRATION_POLICY_INVALID`. A target after the forecast as-of is also `CALIBRATION_FUTURE_LEAK`. The samples and policy ride on the request's `calibration` field; a horizon passes when its Brier is at most `max_brier` and the forecast top-state probability of that horizon is at least `min_top_state_probability`; a failure returns `passed: false`, never a refusal. Depends: R002.11.1, `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R179`, R002.1.10. Shape: `brier_score(probabilities: &ProbabilityMatrix, labels: &[usize])` already computes `mean_i sum_k (p_ik - [y_i = k])^2`, the formula of this row, so no `categorical_brier` is added; the handler builds one `ProbabilityMatrix` per horizon from the held-out samples and compares the result with the policy `RegimeCalibrationPolicy` in `crates/medulla-types/src/regime_forecast.rs`. | `tests/finance_regime_forecast_served.rs::brier_gate_per_horizon` (L3) compares the held-out Brier with the formula and asserts each refusal and a threshold failure. R009.1 flip calibration is untouched. |
Depends: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R179, MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.1, MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.1.10
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.3` | **Policy-bound regime sizing input.** Stage: sizing input. Caller chain: arbora `market.size` (`ARBORA-DATA-MARKET-R003.9`) → signed dispatch `Method::RegimeSizingInput { request, policy, forecast_digest }` → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/terminal.rs` (:20, read chain; new `try_handle_finance` arm) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_regime.rs::handle_regime_sizing_input` (new). Code: `RegimeSizingPolicy {policy_id, revision, instrument_id, target_weight_by_state, unit, fixed_point_scale, risk_cap}`; all weights and the cap share the R010 unit and scale. Rule: expected_target_weight_h = Σ_s p_hs × target_weight_s, then apply the risk cap. Require the forecast digest of R002.11.1 and a passing gate of R002.11.2. Refusal: typed abstention `SIZING_ABSTAIN` (the reason leads the detail) with the reason `POLICY_WEIGHT_MISSING`, `RISK_CAP_EXCEEDED`, `FORECAST_STALE` or `CALIBRATION_FAILED`, and no sizing value. Write path: none; the result is advisory and creates no order. Depends: R002.11.1, R002.11.2, `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R010`. Shape: the handler reads the forecast of R002.11.1 and the gate of R002.11.2 through the same read path, then applies `RegimeSizingPolicy` (new in `crates/medulla-types/src/regime_forecast.rs`); weights and the cap are fixed-point integers in the policy's `unit` at its `fixed_point_scale`, the fixed-point convention of the finance lot engine; the R010 module (`crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/leverage.rs`) declares no scale of its own. A policy with an empty unit, a non-positive scale or a negative cap is `INVALID_ARGUMENT`. The handler recomputes the forecast and gate from `request` and writes nothing; a digest that differs from the recomputed forecast abstains `FORECAST_STALE`. | `tests/finance_regime_forecast_served.rs::sizing_is_deterministic_and_policy_bound` (L3) varies one state probability and shows the weight changes only through the bound policy. It asserts every abstention reason and that no order record exists. |
Depends: MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R010, MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.1, MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.11.2
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R010.2` | **Served order limit verification through the Decide limit guard.** Stage: 3 Policy and constraints, op `Decide`. Caller chain: arbora `market.order.stage` (`ARBORA-DATA-MARKET-R007.3`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_order_proposal.rs::stage` (new) → new `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_order_limit_check.rs::check_order`. Code: `check_order` builds a `GuardedAction` from the order instrument, signed quantity and notional (`crates/medulla-types/src/finance_order.rs`). It runs `crates/medulla-decision-outcome-types/src/decision/limit_guard.rs::check_limits` (:90), the one function `limit_stage::apply` of `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R131.3` also runs, against the pinned policy limits. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R011.2.3` | **Served FinanceAlertRaise door.** Stage: mutation gateway. Entry: `Method::FinanceAlertRaise { envelope: FinanceAlertEnvelope }`. Caller chain: arbora scheduled jobs of `ARBORA-DATA-MARKET-R001` and `R002` → engine client → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/plan.rs::is_gateway_routed` (:564; the method name is added to `GATEWAY_ROUTED`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway.rs::try_handle_gateway` (:57) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway_graph_routes.rs` (`ROUTE_MATCHERS` :131, new `Route::FinanceRecords`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_alerts.rs::raise` (new) → `MutationBatch` compile (`crates/medulla-server-api/src/batch_compile/compile.rs::compile_methods_with_outbox` :140). | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R011.4` | **FinanceReceipt idempotency record.** Stage: record write through `MutationBatch`. Rule: resolve activities at `known_at` and call `lot_accounting::apply_activities`. Apply corporate actions and join valuation. Wrap each output in a `GoldenFixtureFigure` and run `check_figure_provenance` before return. Refusal: a figure without source, as-of or session returns `FIGURE_PROVENANCE_MISSING`; unsupported basis policy returns `BASIS_POLICY_UNSUPPORTED`. Write path: none; the projection is a discardable read model keyed by tenant, account, as-of, input digest and policy version. Depends: R013.1, R005.1, `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R004.3.2`. Shape: `Method::FinanceAccountProject` in `crates/medulla-protocol-types/src/protocol/method/method_04.rs` beside `FinanceFactorRank` (:288), classified as a read in `crates/medulla-server-auth/src/access/read_classification.rs` and scoped `finance:records` (capability line in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/domains/finance.rs`, new); the handler reads `Activity` records through the graph read path, calls `lot_accounting::apply_activities` (:187), wraps each output in `GoldenFixtureFigure` (`golden_fixture.rs:22`) and reuses `check_figure_provenance` (:53); the `h-graph` crate takes `medulla-finance` and `medulla-finance-market` as dependencies behind its `finance` feature. Supported `basis_policy` values are `fifo` and `lifo`; any other value, including `average_cost` and `specific_lot`, answers `BASIS_POLICY_UNSUPPORTED`. Activities are the account's `Activity` records (`activityAccount`); a figure's `source` is the record's `recordSource` and its `session` is `activitySession`, or the `session_at` label of the account's `accountCalendar`. Buys and sells replay through `lot_accounting::apply_activities_from` (the one engine, continued across corporate actions). Figures per position: `open_quantity`, `cost_basis`, `realized_gain`, `market_value` (last traded price, split-adjusted) and `dividend_cash`; a futures roll adds `roll_cash` and `roll_position_value`. Result types `FinanceAccountProjection` and `ProjectedFigure` live in `crates/medulla-types/src/finance_accounting.rs`. The shipped `roll_position` is `crates/medulla-finance/src/futures_roll.rs`. | `tests/finance_golden_served.rs::projection_returns_provenanced_figures` (L3, exists) projects a seeded account through signed dispatch and asserts provenance on every figure and the two refusals. |

Rule: input is the snapshot plus expected head revision and digest. Create a sealed, create-only, content-addressed snapshot; class `OrderProposalSnapshot` is added to `SEALED_RECORD_CLASSES`. It stages revision 2 and retries equal bytes. Each refusal must leave state unchanged. |
| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R015.1.2` | **FinanceOrderProposal.Promote.** Stage: order proposal. Caller chain: arbora `market.order.confirm` (R007.2 of `ARBORA-DATA-MARKET`) → engine client → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-server-mutation/src/mutation/plan.rs::is_gateway_routed` (:564; the method name is added to `GATEWAY_ROUTED`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway.rs::try_handle_gateway` (:57) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/gateway_graph_routes.rs` (`ROUTE_MATCHERS` :131, new `Route::FinanceRecords`) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/finance_order_proposal.rs::promote` (new). Code: request `{tenant_id, proposal_id, candidate_revision, snapshot_digest, expected_promoted_revision, required_gate_manifest_digest, gate_receipts: [{gate_id, gate_revision, candidate_digest, pass_fail, evidence_digest}]}`. Rule: recompute SHA-256 over schema-versioned payload bytes and the staged digest. Confirm decision verification and approval of `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R019`, and the per-instrument risk and sizing policy with designated approvers of R010. That policy is the tenant's `TradingPolicy` record: a typed per-tenant record in the finance store (`TradingPolicy` in `crates/medulla-types/src/finance_order.rs`, fields `tenant_id`, `revision`, `leverage_limits`, `risk_limits`, `designated_approvers: Vec<String>`) that holds the leverage and risk policy and the designated approvers. It is written only through `FinanceRecordPut` with kind `TradingPolicy` (a new `FinanceRecordKind` variant, client-writable, requiring the admin scope `finance:admin` in addition to `finance:records`, and subject to the replicated command of R004.3.2.9). `live_order_eligibility` reads the record in the same read snapshot as the proposal it checks; a tenant with no `TradingPolicy` record refuses `TRADING_POLICY_MISSING` (added to `ServerErrorCode`) and writes nothing. Every gate id of the manifest needs a passing receipt bound to the candidate digest. Refusal: `TRADING_POLICY_MISSING`, `PROMOTE_GATE_UNAVAILABLE`, `PROMOTE_GATE_FAILED`, `PROMOTE_HEAD_STALE`, `PROMOTE_EVIDENCE_MISMATCH`, `PROMOTE_RECEIPT_INCOMPLETE`, `ORDER_PROMOTION_VETOED`. A refusal writes no pointer, history or receipt. Depends: R015.1.1, `ARBORA-DATA-MARKET-R007`, `SCION-GOVERNED-WRITEBACK-R001`. Shape: `promote` verifies the decision with the kernel types of `crates/medulla-decision-outcome-types/src/decision/` (not the sibling handler crate `medulla-h-decide`), calls `crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/leverage.rs::live_order_eligibility` (:54) with the tenant's `TradingPolicy` for the approver check of R010.1, and recomputes the payload digest with the helper `stage` uses; each gate receipt is checked against the manifest digest before any write is built. | `tests/finance_order_proposal_served.rs::promote_refuses_each_case_without_writes` (planned) (L3) covers each refusal code, including `TRADING_POLICY_MISSING` for a tenant with no policy record and a non-admin `TradingPolicy` put refused, and asserts pointer, history and receipt are absent. A passing case with a full manifest writes them atomically. |

Shape: `OrderAdmissionReceipt` is a new sealed class in `SEALED_RECORD_CLASSES` (`sealed_record.rs:57`) and the history edge is the core `derivedFrom`; the pointer, edge and receipt are one `MutationBatch`, so a failure after the pointer write rolls the whole batch back and surfaces as `PROMOTE_WRITE_FAILED`. | `tests/finance_order_proposal_served.rs::admission_receipt_is_atomic_and_effect_free` (planned) (L3) injects a failure after the pointer write and asserts nothing remains. It asserts no order record is created. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R750.751` | **Behavior.** Code: `crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/pattern.rs::classify` (new) and `crates/medulla-finance/src/attribution/brinson.rs::brinson_fachler` (new, beside the feature-attribution modules of `attribution/mod.rs`). Both use plain `f64` in a fixed iteration order, so golden vectors replay on every target. Classify takes parallel `open`, `high`, `low`, `close` slices and sets `range = high - low`, `body = |close - open|`. Doji: `range > 0` and `body <= doji_body_max * range`. Hammer: `body > 0`, lower shadow at least `shadow_ratio_min * body`, upper shadow at most `wick_max * range`. Shooting star mirrors the hammer. Bullish engulfing: the prior bar closes below its open and this bar closes above its open. This open is at or below the prior close. This close is at or above the prior open. Bearish engulfing mirrors it. Inside bar: this high is at most the prior high and this low is at least the prior low. Strength is `body / range` in [0, 1]; for a doji it is `1 - body / range`. Output is ordered by index, then enum order. Knobs: knobs `pattern.doji_body_max` 0.1 ([0.01, 0.3], ratio), `pattern.shadow_ratio_min` 2.0 ([1.5, 4.0], ratio) and `pattern.wick_max` 0.1 ([0, 0.3], ratio). Attribution: with `R_p = sum(w_p * r_p)` and `R_b = sum(w_b * r_b)`, group allocation is `(w_p - w_b) * (r_b - R_b)`, selection is `w_b * (r_p - r_b)`, interaction is `(w_p - w_b) * (r_p - r_b)`. Their sum equals `R_p - R_b` within 1e-12. Caller chain: `crates/medulla-h-compute/src/finance/research_kernels.rs::handle_research_kernels` (new) → `crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/pattern.rs::classify` and `crates/medulla-finance/src/attribution/brinson.rs::brinson_fachler`. Shape: both are pure functions over slices and structs of R750.750 in a fixed iteration order, with the three pattern knobs read from the request-time policy rather than constants. Depends: R750.750. | Test: `crates/medulla-finance-quant/src/pattern.rs::tests::golden_vectors_per_pattern` (planned) and `crates/medulla-finance/src/attribution/brinson.rs::tests::effects_sum_to_active_return` (planned) (L1) assert the vectors and the identity. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R751.751` | **Behavior.** Code: `crates/medulla-finance/src/research.rs::fuse_scores` (new, declared in `crates/medulla-finance/src/lib.rs`). The score is `sum(weight * score) / sum(weight)` over the components, summed in input order. Weights are positive and finite. The write handler computes the score. A caller-supplied score is an unknown field and is refused. Each component `evidenceRef` must resolve to a node of the same tenant. A debate round must carry `roundIndex` equal to the prior round plus one, starting at 1. Bias audits are stored as claims by the submitter; medulla computes no bias finding. A stored record gets a `derivedFrom` edge to every evidence node. Caller chain: the `SentimentFusion` and `GeopoliticalRisk` entries of `finance_records.rs::handle_record` (R751.754) → `crates/medulla-finance/src/research.rs::fuse_scores`. Shape: a pure function over `&[Component]` summed in input order; the evidence-resolution and round-order checks stay in the handler, which reads the graph, so the kernel crate holds no graph dependency. Depends: R751.750. | Test: `crates/medulla-finance/src/research.rs::tests::fusion_matches_hand_computed_vectors` (planned) (L1) asserts three vectors, including a single component. |

A rewrite of a stored record is refused with `RECORD_IMMUTABLE`. Each refusal writes nothing. | `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R900.2` | **Behavior.** `kelly_fraction` returns `(p * b - q) / b`, scaled by the fraction and clamped to [0, max_risk]. A ratio of zero or less returns zero. `distribution_shift` calls `medulla_numeric::stats::ks_2samp` and reports a shift when the statistic exceeds the threshold. Both are pure. Code: `ForecastSample { series_id, model_revision, seed, temperature, top_p, sample_count, values, probabilistic: bool }` in `crates/medulla-types/src/finance_forecast.rs` (new, beside `finance_event_envelope.rs`), always stored with `probabilistic = true`. Rule: the sizing and backtest kernels accept a deterministic series revision only. A `ForecastSample` input is refused with `PROBABILISTIC_INPUT_REFUSED`. A forecast may enter the decision engine as evidence of class `Claim` (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R001.101`) with the premise `forecast:seed=<n>`. Evidence: readback preserves `probabilistic`, the seed and the controls. Writes: the `SourceIngest` commit. Depends: SCION-FINANCE-SOURCES-R901. Code: `BarRecord`, `BarStatus`, `Timeframe` and `TradingCalendar` in `crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bars.rs` (`BarStatus` :17, `BarRecord` :29, `Timeframe` :49, `TradingCalendar` :113) with the calendar logic in `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/calendar.rs`. Rollup takes the first open, the highest high, the lowest low, the last close and the summed volume. Calendar alignment buckets bars by the trading calendar (`calendar.rs`). A rolled bar is `Final` only when every part is final and the watermark has reached the bucket end. A revised bar replaces the prior bar under the same bucket identity. The earlier revision stays readable. Refusal: `OUT_OF_ORDER` (`MarketError`) for a bar that straddles its bucket. Knobs: none. Writes: none; the kernels are pure reads. Depends: MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R014. Shape: no new code; `rollup` calls `calendar::bucket` (:97) and `fixed::add`, refuses a straddling bar with `OUT_OF_ORDER` and keeps the earlier revision readable through `resolve`. | Test: `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/rollup.rs::tests::hourly_bars_roll_up_into_four_hour_bars` (:104), `::an_open_period_or_a_provisional_part_leaves_the_rolled_bar_provisional` (:132), `::a_bar_wider_than_the_target_is_refused` (:165) and `crates/medulla-finance-market/src/market/record_tests.rs::a_revision_appends_revision_records_and_never_rewrites` (:52) (L2, exist). Add the record `WalletKeyTrust { tenant_id, account_id, wallet_id, network_id, key_id, public_key, signer_ref, trust_revision, valid_from, valid_until, revoked }` in `crates/medulla-types/src/finance_wallet_trust.rs` (new, beside `finance_event_envelope.rs`). Serve a read-only `Method::WalletKeyTrustGet` that returns the current record for a binding under the caller's tenant. Writes go through the existing mutation gateway with an owner-approved change. `public_key` is 32 bytes. A cross-tenant read returns not found. Medulla stores the fact; the SDK verifies signatures and decides nothing about trust. Caller chain: SDK wallet verification → engine client → signed dispatch → `crates/medulla-server-router/src/dispatch/router.rs::dispatch_request_method_inner` (:208) → `crates/medulla-server-graph/src/dispatch/graph_pipeline.rs::dispatch_graph_op` (:68) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/terminal.rs` (:20, read chain; new `try_handle_finance` arm) → `crates/medulla-h-graph/src/graph_ops/wallet_trust.rs::get` (new). Shape: `Method::WalletKeyTrustGet { account_id, wallet_id, network_id }` sits beside `FinanceFactorRank` in `method_04.rs` (:288), is classified a read in `crates/medulla-server-auth/src/access/read_classification.rs` and scoped `finance:records` in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/domains/finance.rs` (new); the write path is the existing `AddNode` gateway route (`gateway_graph_routes.rs:226`) with `public_key` length checked to 32 bytes by `WalletKeyTrustShape` in `finance-v1.shapes.ttl`. Depends: none. | Test: `tests/finance_wallet_trust_served.rs::get_returns_current_record_and_refuses_cross_tenant` (planned) (L3). It asserts the record fields, a revoked flag and a cross-tenant not-found. |

| `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R905.1` | **BarRecord/BarStatus is one explicit state machine, and every writer routes through its transition function.** Stage: model, store cutover and ontology (organization constitution Article XXXI). Language: Rust. Census: the `state-machine-shape` hook reports 1 findings for this type (1 SM-NO-DECLARATION). Machine `BarMachine`. States: closed enum `BarStatus` (`crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bars.rs:17`): Provisional, Final. Initial state: the variant a new record takes, declared in the edge table; terminal states: the variants that leave no edge. Events: one closed event per legal edge, named for the verb of the code path that moves the state; the edge table lists them and every state and event pair it does not list is refused. Transition function: `BarStatus::apply(event) -> Result<BarStatus, TransitionRefused>` in new file `crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bar_machine.rs`; it is the only writer of the state and returns `TransitionRefused` for every other pair. Refusal: `TransitionRefused` with fields `machine` (`BarMachine`), `from`, `event` and `reason`, defined by MEDULLA-ORCH-VOCAB-R811.4; an illegal pair changes nothing. Persisted state: one field, `BarRecord.status: BarStatus` (`crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bars.rs:29`); no boolean, nullable timestamp or string flag sits beside it, and any such companion field is derived from the state or deleted. Writers that must route through `BarStatus::apply`: none assign the field after construction today; the writers are the constructors of the record, which build it through `BarStatus::apply` with the initial event. Delete every direct assignment at those sites in the same change; no alias or old path remains. Ontology: add `:BarMachine` of `:StateMachine` to `crates/medulla-core/ontology/state-machines-v1.ttl`, one state instance per variant with its `:storedForm`, one transition per edge with `:fromState`, `:toState`, `:firedBy` and `:guardedBy`, and classes that extend `:State`, `:TerminalState`, `:Transition`, `:TransitionEvent` and `:TransitionGuard` by `rdfs:subClassOf`; no `owl:imports`. Caller chain: the served method that reaches the holder -> the writer functions above -> `BarStatus::apply` -> the stored field. Writes: the state field only. Depends: none. Shape: one machine file, edits at the sites named, Turtle additions. | Test: `crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bar_machine.rs::tests::every_legal_transition_applies` and `crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bar_machine.rs::tests::every_illegal_pair_is_refused_with_transition_refused` and `crates/medulla-result-types/src/compute_result/market/bar_machine.rs::tests::no_writer_sets_status_outside_apply` and `crates/medulla-rdf/tests/state_machine_vocabulary.rs::bar_machine_matches_edge_table` (planned) (L1).
